-- specification EF (input = D_MU_MF & output = OUT_TAlert)  is true
-- specification AG ((input = N_MU_MF & output = OUT_TWarning) -> AX (output = OUT_TWarning | output = OUT_Safe))  is true
-- specification EF (input = N_MU_MF & output = OUT_TWarning)  is false
-- as demonstrated by the following execution sequence

    OUT_None = 1
    OUT_Alert = 0

-- specification EF (input = N_MU_MF & output = OUT_TAlert)  is false
-- as demonstrated by the following execution sequence
